# Commands · P · part 4

1,487 entries, polish to prd-to-issues. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **polish**: Iterative codebase harmonization. Discovers patterns, finds inconsistencies, and unifies code across files. ([rexeus/agentic](https://github.com/rexeus/agentic/blob/HEAD/commands/polish.md) · ★3)
- **polish**: Iteratively raise code quality until a review round comes back clean. Use when the user asks to polish/clean up a module or codebase — 打磨/清理/提升代码质量 — or wants review findings actually fixed, not just listed. Unlike a one-shot code review, every finding is adversarially verified, then fixed and regression-tested, looping until a dry round (no fresh confirmed findings). Behavior-preserving — public-contract changes are reported, never applied. ([sdsrss/loop_eng](https://github.com/sdsrss/loop_eng/blob/HEAD/commands/polish.md) · ★2)
- **polish**: - Always run `git branch --show-current` before any commit. If on main, STOP. - Never use `--no-verify` on git commits. - Conventional commits: `feat(&lt;crate&gt;):`, `fix(&lt;crate&gt;):`, `refactor(&lt;crate&gt;):`. - Cargo gates before committing: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace -- -D warnings`, `cargo nextest run --workspace`. - 3-attempt rule: if a test or fix fails 3 times, stop and report the r ([89jobrien/godmode](https://github.com/89jobrien/godmode/blob/HEAD/commands/gm-polish.md) · ★1)
